===== M0001 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 83-105
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

241 |   private void addToString(int c) {
242 |     int N = stringBufferTop;
243 |     if (N == stringBuffer.length) {
244 |         char[] tmp = new char[stringBuffer.length * 2];
245 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, N);
246 |         stringBuffer = tmp;
247 |     }
248 |     stringBuffer[N] = (char)c;
249 |     stringBufferTop = N + 1;
250 |   }

/**
   * Given a graph G with nodes A and B, this algorithm determines if all paths
   * from A to B contain at least one node satisfying a given predicate.
   *
   * Note that nodePredicate is not necessarily called for all nodes in G nor is
   * edgePredicate called for all edges in G.
   *
   * @param graph Graph G to analyze.
   * @param a The node A.
   * @param b The node B.
   * @param nodePredicate Predicate which at least one node on each path from an
   *     A node to B (inclusive) must match.
   * @param edgePredicate Edges to consider as part of the graph. Edges in
   *     graph that don't match edgePredicate will be ignored.
   * @param inclusive Includes node A and B in the test for the node predicate.
   */
Method code:
85 |   CheckPathsBetweenNodes(DiGraph<N, E> graph, DiGraphNode<N, E> a,
86 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> b, Predicate<N> nodePredicate,
87 |       Predicate<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> edgePredicate, boolean inclusive) {
88 |     this.graph = graph;
89 |     this.start = a;
90 |     this.end = b;
91 |     this.nodePredicate = nodePredicate;
92 |     this.edgePredicate = edgePredicate;
93 |     this.inclusive = inclusive;
94 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Declares a variable.
   *
   * @param name name of the variable
   * @param nameNode the NAME node declaring the variable
   * @param type the variable's type
   * @param input the input in which this variable is defined.
   * @param inferred Whether this variable's type is inferred (as opposed
   *     to declared).
   */
Method code:
498 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,
499 |       JSType type, CompilerInput input, boolean inferred) {
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(name != null && name.length() > 0);
501 | 
502 |     // Make sure that it's declared only once
503 |     Preconditions.checkState(vars.get(name) == null);
504 | 
505 |     // native variables do not have a name node.
506 |     JSDocInfo info = nameNode == null
507 |         ? null : NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(nameNode);
508 | 
509 |     Var var = new Var(inferred, name, nameNode, type, this, vars.size(), input,
510 |         info != null && info.isDefine(), info);
511 | 
512 |     vars.put(name, var);
513 |     return var;
514 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 774-788
Documentation:

Method code:
774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }
788 |   }

567 | 
568 |     if (scope.parent != null && recurse) {
569 |       return scope.parent.isDeclared(name, recurse);
570 |     }
571 |     return false;
572 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.isTry() &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0058 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.GatherNoSideEffectFunctions.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java
Original range: 119-161
Documentation:

Method code:
119 |     @Override
120 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
121 |       if (!inExterns && hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
122 |         traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (node.isGetProp()) {
126 |         if (parent.isExprResult() &&
127 |             hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
128 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.add(node);
129 |         }
130 |       } else if (node.isFunction()) {
131 | 
132 |         // The annotation may attached to the function node, the
133 |         // variable declaration or assignment expression.
134 |         boolean hasAnnotation = hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node);
135 |         List<Node> nameNodes = Lists.newArrayList();
136 |         nameNodes.add(node.getFirstChild());
137 | 
138 |         Node nameNode = null;
139 | 
140 |         if (parent.isName()) {
141 |           Node gramp = parent.getParent();
142 |           if (gramp.isVar() &&
143 |               gramp.hasOneChild() &&
144 |               hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(gramp)) {
145 |             hasAnnotation = true;
146 |           }
147 | 
148 |           nameNodes.add(parent);
149 |         } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
150 |           if (hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(parent)) {
151 |             hasAnnotation = true;
152 |           }
153 | 
154 |           nameNodes.add(parent.getFirstChild());
155 |         }
156 | 
157 |         if (hasAnnotation) {
158 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.addAll(nameNodes);
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 242-305
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
242 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
243 |     this.options = options;
244 |     if (errorManager == null) {
245 |       if (outStream == null) {
246 |         setErrorManager(
247 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
248 |       } else {
249 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
250 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
251 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
252 |         setErrorManager(printer);
253 |       }
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
257 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
258 |       options.checkTypes = true;
259 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
260 |       options.checkTypes = false;
261 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
262 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
263 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
264 |       // parser type warnings.
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
267 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
268 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
272 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
273 |       options.setWarningLevel(
274 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
275 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
279 |       options.setWarningLevel(
280 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
281 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
282 |     }
283 | 
284 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
285 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
286 |     guards.add(
287 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
288 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
289 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
290 | 
291 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
292 | 
293 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
294 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
295 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
296 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
297 |     // error off.
298 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
299 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
300 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
301 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
305 |   }

===== M0089 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 828-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
828 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
829 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
830 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
831 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
832 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
833 |           return;
834 |         }
835 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
836 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
837 | 
838 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
839 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
840 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
841 |           } else {
842 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           }
844 |         } else { // Has a catch.
845 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
846 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
847 |             return;
848 |           } else {
849 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
850 |           }
851 |         }
852 |         lastJump = handler;
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
454 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
455 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
456 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
457 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
458 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
459 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
460 |         String inputName = input.getName();
461 | 
462 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
463 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
464 |         // will get an error down the line.
465 |         inputs.add(input);
466 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |     return inputs;
470 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 637-664
Documentation:

Method code:
637 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
638 |     Node lastJump = null;
639 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
640 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
641 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
642 |         break;
643 |       }
644 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
645 |         if (lastJump == null) {
646 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
647 |         } else {
648 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
649 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
650 |         }
651 |         lastJump = curHandler;
652 |       }
653 |     }
654 | 
655 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
656 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     if (lastJump == null) {
660 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
661 |     } else {
662 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
663 |     }
664 |   }

155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:

163 |         return;
164 |

166 |       case Token.DELPROP:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.NEG:
169 |       case Token.NOT:
170 |       case Token.INC:
171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:

196 |       case Token.RSH:

199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:

204 |         return;
205 |

207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:

220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 |

237 |         return;

240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;

244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:

261 |         return;
262 |

265 |         return;

269 |         return;
270 |

273 |             + Token.name(n.getType()), n);
274 |     }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

Method code:
463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

===== M0157 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.IR.mayBeStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/IR.java
Original range: 466-496
Documentation:
/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
466 |   private static boolean mayBeStatement(Node n) {
467 |     switch (n.getType()) {
468 |       case Token.EMPTY:
469 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
470 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
471 |         // contexts
472 |         return true;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.BLOCK:
475 |       case Token.BREAK:
476 |       case Token.CONST:
477 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
478 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
479 |       case Token.DO:
480 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
481 |       case Token.FOR:
482 |       case Token.IF:
483 |       case Token.LABEL:
484 |       case Token.RETURN:
485 |       case Token.SWITCH:
486 |       case Token.THROW:
487 |       case Token.TRY:
488 |       case Token.VAR:
489 |       case Token.WHILE:
490 |       case Token.WITH:
491 |         return true;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         return false;
495 |     }
496 |   }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 616-698
Documentation:

Method code:
616 |     @Override
617 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
618 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
619 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
620 |       if (name == null) {
621 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
622 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
623 |           errorReporter.error(
624 |             "unnamed function statement",
625 |             sourceName,
626 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
627 | 
628 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
629 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
630 |         }
631 |         name = new Name();
632 |         name.setIdentifier("");
633 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
634 |       }
635 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
636 |       Node newName = transform(name);
637 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
638 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
639 |         // declaration.
640 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
641 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
642 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
643 |         // same line as the function keyword.
644 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
645 |             functionNode.getLp();
646 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
647 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
648 |       }
649 | 
650 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
651 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
652 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
653 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
654 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
655 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
656 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
657 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
658 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
659 |       if (fnName != null) {
660 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
661 |       } else {
662 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
663 |       }
664 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
665 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
666 | 
667 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
668 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
669 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
670 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
671 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
672 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
673 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
674 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
675 |         } else {
676 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
677 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
678 |           // (an error has already been reported).
679 |           Preconditions.checkState(
680 |               config.isIdeMode
681 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
682 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
683 |         }
684 |       }
685 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
686 | 
687 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
688 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
689 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
690 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
691 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
693 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
694 |       }
695 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
696 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
697 |      return node;
698 |     }

119 |     public MustDef(Iterator<Var> vars) {
120 |       this();
121 |       while(vars.hasNext()) {
122 |         Var var = vars.next();
123 |         // Every variable in the scope is defined once in the beginning of the
124 |         // function: all the declared variables are undefined, all functions

===== M0176 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0183 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0189 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 987-1043
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
987 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
988 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
989 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
990 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
991 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
992 |     }
993 | 
994 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
995 |       return false;
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
999 | 
1000 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1001 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1002 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1003 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1004 |         return false;
1005 |       }
1006 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1007 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1008 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1009 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1010 |         return false;
1011 |       }
1012 | 
1013 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1014 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1015 |         return false;
1016 |       }
1017 | 
1018 |       // Math.floor has no sideeffects.
1019 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1020 |       // I create a definitionprovider that understands namespacing.
1021 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1022 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1023 |           return false;
1024 |         }
1025 |       }
1026 | 
1027 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1028 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1029 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1030 |           return false;
1031 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1032 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1033 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1034 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1035 |           if (param != null &&
1036 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp()))
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         }
1039 |       }
1040 |     }
1041 | 
1042 |     return true;
1043 |   }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1095-1115
Documentation:

Method code:
1095 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1096 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1097 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1098 |       return s;
1099 |     }
1100 | 
1101 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1102 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1103 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1104 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1105 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1106 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1107 |       // crazy.
1108 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1109 |         sb.append(c);
1110 |       } else {
1111 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1112 |       }
1113 |     }
1114 |     return sb.toString();
1115 |   }

   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping

Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2399-2409
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2399 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2400 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2401 |     int len = s.length();
2402 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2403 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2404 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2405 |         return false;
2406 |       }
2407 |     }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 267-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Performs a single flow through a node.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if the flow state differs from the previous state.
   */
Method code:
267 |   protected boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
268 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
269 |     if (isForward()) {
270 |       L outBefore = state.out;
271 |       state.out = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
272 |       return !outBefore.equals(state.out);
273 |     } else {
274 |       L inBefore = state.in;
275 |       state.in = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.out);
276 |       return !inBefore.equals(state.in);
277 |     }
278 |   }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 286-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the new flow state at a given node's entry by merging the
   * output (input) lattice of the node's predecessor (successor).
   *
   * @param node Node to compute new join.
   */
Method code:
286 |   protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
287 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
288 |     if (isForward()) {
289 |       if (cfg.getEntry() == node) {
290 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
291 |       } else {
292 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(node);
293 |         if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
294 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNodes.get(0).getAnnotation();
295 |           state.setIn(inNodeState.getOut());
296 |         } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
297 |           List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
298 |           for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
299 |             FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
300 |             values.add(currentNodeState.getOut());
301 |           }
302 |           state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     } else {
306 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(node);
307 |       if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
308 |         DiGraphNode<N, Branch> inNode = inNodes.get(0);
309 |         if (inNode == cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
310 |           state.setOut(createEntryLattice());
311 |         } else {
312 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNode.getAnnotation();
313 |           state.setOut(inNodeState.getIn());
314 |         }
315 |       } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
316 |         List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
317 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
318 |           FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
319 |           values.add(currentNodeState.getIn());
320 |         }
321 |         state.setOut(joinOp.apply(values));
322 |       }
323 |     }
324 |   }

===== M0250 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.checkLeftOf(Node,Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 480-491
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the left of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * Checked(), Checked(), n, NotChecked(), NotChecked();
   */
Method code:
480 |   private static boolean checkLeftOf(
481 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
482 |     for (Node p = n.getParent(); p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
483 |       for (Node cur = p.getParent().getFirstChild(); cur != p;
484 |           cur = cur.getNext()) {
485 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
486 |           return true;
487 |         }
488 |       }
489 |     }
490 |     return false;
491 |   }

===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-267
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if ((long) x == x && !negativeZero) {
250 |       long value = (long) x;
251 |       long mantissa = value;
252 |       int exp = 0;
253 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
254 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
255 |           mantissa /= 10;
256 |           exp++;
257 |         }
258 |       }
259 |       if (exp > 2) {
260 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
261 |       } else {
262 |         add(Long.toString(value));
263 |       }
264 |     } else {
265 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
266 |     }
267 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.

   * @see #analyze()

127 |     } else {

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource
381 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
382 | 
383 |         Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
384 | 
385 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {
390 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
391 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
392 |             throw new RuntimeException
393 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
394 |         }
395 | 
396 |         /*
397 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
398 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
399 |          * single 's.
400 |          */
401 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
402 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
403 |         return formatter.format(arguments);

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 489-506
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.getDefinition(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 403-426
Documentation:
/**
     * Set the def node
     *
     * @param n A node that has a corresponding CFG node in the CFG.
     */
Method code:
403 |     private void getDefinition(Node n, Node parent) {
404 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
405 |         new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
406 | 
407 |         @Override
408 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
409 |           switch (n.getType()) {
410 |             case Token.NAME:
411 |               if (n.getString().equals(varName) && n.hasChildren()) {
412 |                 def = n;
413 |               }
414 |               return;
415 | 
416 |             case Token.ASSIGN:
417 |               Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
418 |               if (lhs.isName() && lhs.getString().equals(varName)) {
419 |                 def = n;
420 |               }
421 |               return;
422 |           }
423 |         }
424 |       };
425 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, n, gatherCb);
426 |     }

===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 898-925
Documentation:

Method code:
898 |     @Override
899 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
900 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
901 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
902 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
903 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
904 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
905 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
906 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
907 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
908 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
909 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
910 |         //
911 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
912 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
913 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
914 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
915 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
916 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
917 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
918 |             (sourceString.substring(
919 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
920 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
921 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
922 |         }
923 |       }
924 |       return n;
925 |     }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.anonymous_L82.apply(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 83-108
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |       @Override
84 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
85 |         // When the node is null it means, we reached the implicit return
86 |         // where the function returns (possibly without an return statement)
87 |         if (n == null) {
88 |           return false;
89 |         }
90 | 
91 |         // TODO(user): We only care about calls to functions that
92 |         // passes one of the dependent variable to a non-sideeffect free
93 |         // function.
94 |         if (n.isCall() && NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
95 |           return true;
96 |         }
97 | 
98 |         if (n.isNew() && NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
99 |           return true;
100 |         }
101 | 
102 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
103 |           if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && apply(c)) {
104 |             return true;
105 |           }
106 |         }
107 |         return false;
108 |       }

===== M0316 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getParserConfig()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1934-1959
Documentation:

Method code:
1934 |   @Override
1935 |   Config getParserConfig() {
1936 |     if (parserConfig == null) {
1937 |       Config.LanguageMode mode;
1938 |       switch (options.getLanguageIn()) {
1939 |         case ECMASCRIPT3:
1940 |           mode = Config.LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
1941 |           break;
1942 |         case ECMASCRIPT5:
1943 |           mode = Config.LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5;
1944 |           break;
1945 |         case ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT:

1947 |           break;
1948 |         default:

1950 |       }

1953 |         isIdeMode(),
1954 |         mode,
1955 |         acceptConstKeyword(),
1956 |         options.extraAnnotationNames);
1957 |     }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
280 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {
281 |     return null;
282 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 739-755
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
739 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
740 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
741 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
742 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
743 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
744 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
745 |     }
746 | 
747 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
748 | 
749 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
750 |     do {
751 |       current = current.getParent();
752 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
753 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
754 |     } while (current != n);
755 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 115-150
Documentation:

Method code:
115 |   @Override
116 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
117 | 
118 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
119 |       return; // Don't even brother. All global variables are likely escaped.
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     if (LiveVariablesAnalysis.MAX_VARIABLES_TO_ANALYZE <
123 |         t.getScope().getVarCount()) {
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     // Compute the forward reaching definition.
128 |     ControlFlowAnalysis cfa = new ControlFlowAnalysis(compiler, false, true);
129 |     // Process the body of the function.
130 |     Preconditions.checkState(t.getScopeRoot().isFunction());
131 |     cfa.process(null, t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
132 |     cfg = cfa.getCfg();
133 |     reachingDef = new MustBeReachingVariableDef(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
134 |     reachingDef.analyze();
135 |     candidates = Lists.newLinkedList();
136 | 
137 |     // Using the forward reaching definition search to find all the inline
138 |     // candiates
139 |     new NodeTraversal(compiler, new GatherCandiates()).traverse(
140 |         t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
141 | 
142 |     // Compute the backward reaching use. The CFG can be reused.
143 |     reachingUses = new MaybeReachingVariableUse(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
144 |     reachingUses.analyze();
145 |     for (Candidate c : candidates) {
146 |       if (c.canInline()) {
147 |         c.inlineVariable();
148 |       }
149 |     }
150 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0369 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 794-807
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }
