===== M0376 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.GatherCandiates.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 177-219
Documentation:

Method code:
177 |     @Override
178 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
179 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> graphNode = cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(n);
180 |       if (graphNode == null) {
181 |         // Not a CFG node.
182 |         return;
183 |       }
184 |       FlowState<MustDef> state = graphNode.getAnnotation();
185 |       final MustDef defs = state.getIn();
186 |       final Node cfgNode = n;
187 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
188 |           new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
189 | 
190 |         @Override
191 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
192 |           if (n.isName()) {
193 | 
194 |             // Make sure that the name node is purely a read.
195 |             if ((NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
196 |                 || parent.isVar() || parent.isInc() ||
197 |                 parent.isDec() || parent.isParamList() ||
198 |                 parent.isCatch()) {
199 |               return;
200 |             }
201 | 
202 |             String name = n.getString();
203 |             if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(name)) {
204 |               return;
205 |             }
206 | 
207 |             Node defNode = reachingDef.getDef(name, cfgNode);
208 |             // TODO(nicksantos): We need to add some notion of @const outer
209 |             // scope vars. We can inline those just fine.
210 |             if (defNode != null &&
211 |                 !reachingDef.dependsOnOuterScopeVars(name, cfgNode)) {
212 |               candidates.add(new Candidate(name, defNode, n, cfgNode));
213 |             }
214 |           }
215 |         }
216 |       };
217 | 
218 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, cfgNode, gatherCb);
219 |     }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.computeMustDef(Node,Node,MustDef,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 224-317
Documentation:
/**
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param cfgNode The node to add
   * @param conditional true if the definition is not always executed.
   */
Method code:
224 |   private void computeMustDef(
225 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, MustDef output, boolean conditional) {
226 |     switch (n.getType()) {
227 | 
228 |       case Token.BLOCK:
229 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
230 |         return;
231 | 
232 |       case Token.WHILE:
233 |       case Token.DO:
234 |       case Token.IF:
235 |         computeMustDef(
236 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.FOR:
240 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
241 |           computeMustDef(
242 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
243 |         } else {
244 |           // for(x in y) {...}
245 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
246 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
247 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
248 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
249 |           }
250 |           if (lhs.isName()) {
251 |             addToDefIfLocal(lhs.getString(), cfgNode, rhs, output);
252 |           }
253 |         }
254 |         return;
255 | 
256 |       case Token.AND:
257 |       case Token.OR:
258 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
259 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
260 |         return;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.HOOK:
263 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
264 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
265 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
266 |         return;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
270 |           if (c.hasChildren()) {
271 |             computeMustDef(c.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
272 |             addToDefIfLocal(c.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
273 |                 c.getFirstChild(), output);
274 |           }
275 |         }
276 |         return;
277 | 
278 |       default:
279 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)) {
280 |           if (n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
281 |             Node name = n.getFirstChild();
282 |             computeMustDef(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
283 |             addToDefIfLocal(name.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
284 |               n.getLastChild(), output);
285 |             return;
286 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isGet(n.getFirstChild())) {
287 |             // Treat all assignments to arguments as redefining the
288 |             // parameters itself.
289 |             Node obj = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
290 |             if (obj.isName() && "arguments".equals(obj.getString())) {
291 |               // TODO(user): More accuracy can be introduced
292 |               // ie: We know exactly what arguments[x] is if x is a constant
293 |               // number.
294 |               escapeParameters(output);
295 |             }
296 |           }
297 |         }
298 | 
299 |         if (n.isName() && "arguments".equals(n.getString())) {
300 |           escapeParameters(output);
301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         // DEC and INC actually defines the variable.
304 |         if (n.isDec() || n.isInc()) {
305 |           Node target = n.getFirstChild();
306 |           if (target.isName()) {
307 |             addToDefIfLocal(target.getString(),
308 |                 conditional ? null : cfgNode, null, output);
309 |             return;
310 |           }
311 |         }
312 | 
313 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
314 |           computeMustDef(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
315 |         }
316 |     }
317 |   }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.inlineVariable()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 375-396
Documentation:
/**
     * Actual transformation.
     */
Method code:
375 |     private void inlineVariable() {
376 |       Node defParent = def.getParent();
377 |       Node useParent = use.getParent();
378 |       if (def.isAssign()) {
379 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
380 |         rhs.detachFromParent();
381 |         // Oh yes! I have grandparent to remove this.
382 |         Preconditions.checkState(defParent.isExprResult());
383 |         while (defParent.getParent().isLabel()) {
384 |           defParent = defParent.getParent();
385 |         }
386 |         defParent.detachFromParent();
387 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
388 |       } else if (defParent.isVar()) {
389 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
390 |         def.removeChild(rhs);
391 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
392 |       } else {
393 |         Preconditions.checkState(false, "No other definitions can be inlined.");
394 |       }
395 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
396 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 489-512
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
489 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
490 |     switch (n.getType()) {
491 |       case Token.STRING:
492 |       case Token.NUMBER:
493 |       case Token.NULL:
494 |       case Token.TRUE:
495 |       case Token.FALSE:
496 |         return true;
497 |       case Token.NOT:
498 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
499 |       case Token.VOID:
500 |       case Token.NEG:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.NAME:
503 |         String name = n.getString();
504 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
505 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
506 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
507 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
508 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
509 |     }
510 | 
511 |     return false;
512 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 595-609
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 |

607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 908-929
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
908 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
909 |     switch (n.getType()) {
910 |       case Token.CALL:
911 |       case Token.GETPROP:
912 |       case Token.GETELEM:
913 |       case Token.THROW:
914 |       case Token.NEW:
915 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
916 |       case Token.INC:
917 |       case Token.DEC:
918 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
919 |         return true;
920 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
921 |         return false;
922 |     }
923 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
924 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
925 |         return true;
926 |       }
927 |     }
928 |     return false;
929 |   }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 460-475
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
460 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
461 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
462 |       Set<String> directives = null;
463 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
464 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
465 |         if (directives == null) {
466 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
467 |         } else {
468 |           directives.add(directive);
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       if (directives != null) {
473 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0490 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 690-787
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
690 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
691 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
692 |     /*
693 |      * This is the case where:
694 |      *
695 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
696 |      * the script.
697 |      *
698 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
699 |      * the caller of the function.
700 |      *
701 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
702 |      * back to the caller of the function.
703 |      *
704 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
705 |      * asked to traverse.
706 |      *
707 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
708 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
709 |      */
710 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
711 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
712 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
713 |       return null;
714 |     }
715 | 
716 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
717 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
718 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
719 |       case Token.IF:
720 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
723 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
724 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
725 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
726 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
727 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
728 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
730 |           } else {
731 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
732 |           }
733 |         } else {
734 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
735 |         }
736 |         break;
737 |       case Token.FOR:
738 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
739 |           return parent;
740 |         } else {
741 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
742 |         }
743 |       case Token.WHILE:
744 |       case Token.DO:
745 |         return parent;
746 |       case Token.TRY:
747 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
748 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
749 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
750 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
751 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
752 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
753 |           }
754 |         // CATCH block.
755 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
756 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
757 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
758 |           } else {
759 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
760 |           }
761 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
762 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
763 |           if (cfa != null) {
764 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
765 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
766 |             }
767 |           }
768 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
769 |         }
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
773 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
774 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
775 | 
776 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
777 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
778 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
782 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
783 |     } else {
784 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
785 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
786 |     }
787 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
135 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
136 |     if (node.isName()) {
137 |       String name = node.getString();
138 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
139 |         return name;
140 |       } else {
141 |         return null;
142 |       }
143 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
144 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
145 |     }
146 |     return null;
147 |   }

===== M0496 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1236-1386
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1236 |   Node parseInputs() {
1237 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1238 | 
1239 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1240 |     // individual file parse trees.
1241 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1242 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1243 |     }
1244 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1245 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1246 |     }
1247 | 
1248 |     // Parse main js sources.
1249 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1250 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1251 | 
1252 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1253 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1254 | 
1255 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1256 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1257 | 
1258 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1259 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1260 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1261 |     }
1262 | 
1263 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1264 | 
1265 |     try {
1266 |       // Parse externs sources.
1267 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1268 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1269 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1270 |           return null;
1271 |         }
1272 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1273 |       }
1274 | 
1275 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1276 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1277 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1278 |       }
1279 | 
1280 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1281 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1282 | 
1283 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1284 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1285 |           options.closurePass) {
1286 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1287 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1288 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1289 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1290 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1291 |           }
1292 |         }
1293 | 
1294 |         try {
1295 |           inputs =
1296 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1297 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1298 |           staleInputs = true;
1299 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1300 |           report(JSError.make(
1301 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1302 | 
1303 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1304 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1305 |             return null;
1306 |           }
1307 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1308 |           report(JSError.make(
1309 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1310 | 
1311 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1312 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1313 |             return null;
1314 |           }
1315 |         }
1316 |       }
1317 | 
1318 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1319 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1320 | 
1321 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1322 |         if (n == null) {
1323 |           continue;
1324 |         }
1325 | 
1326 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1327 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1328 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1329 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1330 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1331 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1332 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1333 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1334 | 
1335 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1336 | 
1337 |             externs.add(input);
1338 |             staleInputs = true;
1339 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1340 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1341 |             staleInputs = true;
1342 |           }
1343 |         }
1344 |       }
1345 | 
1346 |       if (staleInputs) {
1347 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1348 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1349 |       }
1350 | 
1351 |       // Build the AST.
1352 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1353 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1354 |         if (n == null) {
1355 |           continue;
1356 |         }
1357 | 
1358 |         if (devMode) {
1359 |           runSanityCheck();
1360 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1361 |             return null;
1362 |           }
1363 |         }
1364 | 
1365 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1366 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1367 | 
1368 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1369 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1370 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1371 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1372 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1373 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1374 |         }
1375 | 
1376 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1380 |         return null;
1381 |       }
1382 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1383 |     } finally {
1384 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1385 |     }
1386 |   }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)

Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
399 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
400 |       Node ret;
401 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
402 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
403 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
404 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       } else {
407 |         ret = transform(n);
408 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
409 |       }
410 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
411 |       return ret;
412 |     }

684 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,

701 |     CodeGenerator cg =
702 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
703 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
704 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
705 | 
706 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
707 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
708 |     }
709 | 
710 |     cg.add(root);
711 |     mcp.endFile();
712 | 
713 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
714 | 
715 |     if (createSourceMap) {
716 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     return code;
720 |   }

Method code:
93 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
94 |     switch (n.getType()) {
95 |       case Token.LABEL:
96 |         validateLabel(n);
97 |         return;
98 |       case Token.BLOCK:
99 |         validateBlock(n);
100 |         return;
101 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
102 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
103 |         return;
104 |       case Token.WITH:
105 |         validateWith(n);
106 |         return;
107 |       case Token.FOR:
108 |         validateFor(n);
109 |         return;
110 |       case Token.WHILE:
111 |         validateWhile(n);
112 |         return;
113 |       case Token.DO:
114 |         validateDo(n);
115 |         return;
116 |       case Token.SWITCH:
117 |         validateSwitch(n);
118 |         return;
119 |       case Token.IF:
120 |         validateIf(n);
121 |         return;
122 |       case Token.VAR:
123 |         validateVar(n);
124 |         return;
125 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
126 |         validateExprStmt(n);
127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:
150 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
152 |     }
153 |   }

===== M0541 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckPathsBetweenNodes.checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E>,DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckPathsBetweenNodes.java
Original range: 199-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Verify that some non-looping paths from {@code a} to {@code b} pass
   * through at least one node where {@code nodePredicate} is true.
   */
Method code:
199 |   private boolean checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> a,
200 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> b) {
201 |     if (nodePredicate.apply(a.getValue()) &&
202 |         (inclusive || (a != start && a != end))) {
203 |       return true;
204 |     }
205 |     if (a == b) {
206 |       return false;
207 |     }
208 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : a.getOutEdges()) {
209 |       // Once we visited that edge once, we no longer need to
210 |       // re-visit it again.
211 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == VISITED_EDGE) {
212 |         continue;
213 |       }
214 |       e.setAnnotation(VISITED_EDGE);
215 | 
216 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
217 |         continue;
218 |       }
219 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == BACK_EDGE) {
220 |         continue;
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> next = e.getDestination();
224 |       if (checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(next, b)) {
225 |         return true;
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 |     return false;
229 |   }

===== M0544 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0570 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 156-260
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |     @Override
157 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
158 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
159 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
160 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
161 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
162 |         return;
163 |       }
164 | 
165 |       Definition def =
166 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
167 |       if (def != null) {
168 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
169 |         if (name != null) {
170 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
171 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
172 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
173 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
174 | 
175 |             // Unhandled complex expression
176 |             Definition unknownDef =
177 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
178 |             def = unknownDef;
179 |           }
180 | 
181 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
182 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
183 |           if (inExterns) {
184 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
185 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
186 | 
187 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
188 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
189 | 
190 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
191 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
192 |             // literal key.
193 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
194 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
195 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
196 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
197 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
198 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
199 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
200 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
201 |                   }
202 |                 }
203 |               }
204 | 
205 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
206 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
207 |               }
208 |             }
209 |           }
210 | 
211 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
212 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
213 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
214 |                                                    def,
215 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
216 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
217 |                                                    inExterns));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
222 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
223 |         if (name != null) {
224 | 
225 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
226 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
227 | 
228 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
229 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
230 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
231 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
232 | 
233 |           boolean dropStub = false;
234 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
235 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
236 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
237 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
238 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
239 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
240 |                   dropStub = true;
241 |                   break;
242 |                 }
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |           }
246 | 
247 |           if (!dropStub) {
248 |             // Incomplete definition
249 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
250 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
251 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
252 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
253 |                                                      definition,
254 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
255 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
256 |                                                      inExterns));
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.

Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {

1224 |       }
1225 |     }

===== M0576 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0591 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.computeEscaped(Scope,Set<Var>,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 548-578
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute set of escaped variables. When a variable is escaped in a
   * dataflow analysis, it can be reference outside of the code that we are
   * analyzing. A variable is escaped if any of the following is true:
   *
   * <p><ol>
   * <li>It is defined as the exception name in CATCH clause so it became a
   * variable local not to our definition of scope.</li>
   * <li>Exported variables as they can be needed after the script terminates.
   * </li>
   * <li>Names of named functions because in javascript, <i>function foo(){}</i>
   * does not kill <i>foo</i> in the dataflow.</li>
   */
Method code:
548 |   static void computeEscaped(final Scope jsScope, final Set<Var> escaped,
549 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
550 |     // TODO(user): Very good place to store this information somewhere.
551 |     AbstractPostOrderCallback finder = new AbstractPostOrderCallback() {
552 |       @Override
553 |       public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
554 |         if (jsScope == t.getScope() || !n.isName()
555 |             || parent.isFunction()) {
556 |           return;
557 |         }
558 |         String name = n.getString();
559 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
560 |         if (var != null && var.scope == jsScope) {
561 |           escaped.add(jsScope.getVar(name));
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 |     };
565 | 
566 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, finder);
567 |     t.traverseAtScope(jsScope);
568 | 
569 |     // 1: Remove the exception name in CATCH which technically isn't local to
570 |     //    begin with.
571 |     for (Iterator<Var> i = jsScope.getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
572 |       Var var = i.next();
573 |       if (var.getParentNode().isCatch() ||
574 |           compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.getName())) {
575 |         escaped.add(var);
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 |   }

===== M0605 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

2657 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2658 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2659 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2660 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {

2672 |     }

===== M0614 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1555
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1526 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1527 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1529 |         return false;
1530 |       }
1531 | 
1532 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1533 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1534 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1535 |         return false;
1536 |       }
1537 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1538 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1539 |         return false;
1540 |       }
1541 |     }
1542 | 
1543 |     if (recurse) {
1544 |       Node n, n2;
1545 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1546 |            n != null;
1547 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1548 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1549 |           return false;
1550 |         }
1551 |       }
1552 |     }
1553 | 
1554 |     return true;
1555 |   }

===== M0637 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1565-1579
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1565 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1566 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1567 |       return getString();
1568 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1569 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1570 |       if (left == null) {
1571 |         return null;
1572 |       }
1573 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1574 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1575 |       return "this";
1576 |     } else {
1577 |       return null;
1578 |     }
1579 |   }

===== M0645 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 965-992
Documentation:

Method code:
965 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
966 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
967 | 
968 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
969 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
970 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
971 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
972 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
973 |       }
974 |     }
975 | 
976 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
977 |     char quote;
978 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
979 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
980 |       quote = '\'';
981 |       doublequote = "\"";
982 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
983 |     } else {
984 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
985 |       quote = '\"';
986 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
987 |       singlequote = "\'";
988 |     }
989 | 
990 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
991 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
992 |   }

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.addToDefIfLocal(String,Node,Node,MustDef)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 326-357
Documentation:
/**
   * Set the variable lattice for the given name to the node value in the def
   * lattice. Do nothing if the variable name is one of the escaped variable.
   *
   * @param node The CFG node where the definition should be record to.
   *     {@code null} if this is a conditional define.
   */
Method code:
326 |   private void addToDefIfLocal( String name, @Nullable Node node,
327 |       @Nullable Node rValue, MustDef def) {
328 |     Var var = jsScope.getVar(name);
329 | 
330 |     // var might be null because the variable might be defined in the extern
331 |     // that we might not traverse.
332 |     if (var == null || var.scope != jsScope) {
333 |       return;
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     for (Var other : def.reachingDef.keySet()) {
337 |       Definition otherDef = def.reachingDef.get(other);
338 |       if (otherDef == null) {
339 |         continue;
340 |       }
341 |       if (otherDef.depends.contains(var)) {
342 |         def.reachingDef.put(other, null);
343 |       }
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     if (!escaped.contains(var)) {
347 |       if (node == null) {
348 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, null);
349 |       } else {
350 |         Definition definition = new Definition(node);
351 |         if (rValue != null) {
352 |           computeDependence(definition, rValue);
353 |         }
354 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, definition);
355 |       }
356 |     }
357 |   }

61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.

713 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
794 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
795 |     switch (n.getType()) {
796 |       case Token.DO:
797 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
798 |       case Token.FOR:
799 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
800 |           return n;
801 |         }
802 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
803 |       case Token.LABEL:
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
805 |       default:
806 |         return n;
807 |     }
808 |   }

===== M0687 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3003-3029
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3003 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3004 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3005 |     if (info == null) {
3006 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3007 |       if (parent == null) {
3008 |         return null;
3009 |       }
3010 | 
3011 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3012 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3013 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3014 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3015 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3022 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3023 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3024 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3025 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3026 |       }
3027 |     }
3028 |     return info;
3029 |   }

===== M0702 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0710 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0716 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 341-367
Documentation:

Method code:
341 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
342 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
343 |     // two cases:
344 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
345 |     // and
346 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
347 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
348 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
349 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
350 |     //
351 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
352 |     //
353 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
354 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
355 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
356 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
357 |     //
358 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
359 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
360 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
361 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
362 | 
363 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
364 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
365 |     }
366 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
367 |   }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 181-949
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
181 |   boolean parse() {
182 |     int lineno;
183 |     int charno;
184 | 
185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
186 |     JSTypeExpression type;
187 | 
188 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
189 |     skipEOLs();
190 | 
191 |     JsDocToken token = next();
192 | 
193 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
194 | 
195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {
214 |         case ANNOTATION:
215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
217 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {
226 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
228 | 
229 |               switch (annotation) {
230 |                 case AUTHOR:
231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
234 | 
235 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     } else {
239 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
240 |                     }
241 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
242 |                   } else {
243 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
244 |                   }
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;
262 | 
263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                     } else {
269 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
270 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
271 |                     }
272 |                   }
273 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
274 |                   continue retry;
275 | 
276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
278 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 | 
282 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
289 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
290 |                   }
291 | 
292 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
293 |                   continue retry;
294 | 
295 |                 case INTERFACE:
296 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {

942 |             continue retry;
943 |           }
944 |       }
945 | 
946 |       // next token
947 |       token = next();

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:

111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);

125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);

129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }
