===== M0747 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 784-927
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
784 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
785 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
786 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
787 |     // that we know to be safe
788 |     switch (n.getType()) {
789 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
790 |       case Token.AND:
791 |       case Token.BLOCK:
792 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
793 |       case Token.HOOK:
794 |       case Token.IF:
795 |       case Token.IN:
796 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
797 |       case Token.NUMBER:
798 |       case Token.OR:
799 |       case Token.THIS:
800 |       case Token.TRUE:
801 |       case Token.FALSE:
802 |       case Token.NULL:
803 |       case Token.STRING:
804 |       case Token.SWITCH:
805 |       case Token.TRY:
806 |       case Token.EMPTY:
807 |         break;
808 | 
809 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
810 |       case Token.THROW:
811 |         return true;
812 | 
813 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
814 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
815 |           return true;
816 |         }
817 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
818 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
819 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
820 |             return true;
821 |           }
822 |         }
823 |         return false;
824 | 
825 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
826 |       case Token.REGEXP:
827 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
828 |           return true;
829 |         }
830 |         break;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
833 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
834 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
840 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
841 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
842 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
843 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
844 | 
845 |       case Token.NEW:
846 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
847 |           return true;
848 |         }
849 | 
850 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
851 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
852 |           // side-effects
853 |           break;
854 |         }
855 |         return true;
856 | 
857 |       case Token.CALL:
858 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
859 |         // side effect property set.
860 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
861 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
862 |           // side-effects
863 |           break;
864 |         }
865 |         return true;
866 | 
867 |       default:
868 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
869 |           break;
870 |         }
871 | 
872 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
873 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
874 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
875 |             return true;
876 |           }
877 | 
878 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
879 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
880 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
881 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
882 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
883 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
884 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
885 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
886 |             return true;
887 |           }
888 | 
889 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
890 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
891 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
892 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
893 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
894 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
895 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
896 |               return false;
897 |             }
898 | 
899 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
900 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
901 |             // other objects.
902 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
903 |             // side-effect.
904 |             while (isGet(current)) {
905 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
906 |             }
907 | 
908 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
909 |           } else {
910 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
911 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
912 |             // preserve existing behavior.
913 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         return true;
918 |     }
919 | 
920 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
921 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
922 |         return true;
923 |       }
924 |     }
925 | 
926 |     return false;
927 |   }

175 |     @Override
176 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
177 |       if (!node.isCall() && !node.isNew()) {
178 |         return;
179 |       }

===== M0759 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MaybeReachingVariableUse.computeMayUse(Node,Node,ReachingUses,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MaybeReachingVariableUse.java
Original range: 152-237
Documentation:

Method code:
152 |   private void computeMayUse(
153 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, ReachingUses output, boolean conditional) {
154 |     switch (n.getType()) {
155 | 
156 |       case Token.BLOCK:
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         return;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.NAME:
161 |         addToUseIfLocal(n.getString(), cfgNode, output);
162 |         return;
163 | 
164 |       case Token.WHILE:
165 |       case Token.DO:
166 |       case Token.IF:
167 |         computeMayUse(
168 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
169 |         return;
170 | 
171 |       case Token.FOR:
172 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
173 |           computeMayUse(
174 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
175 |         } else {
176 |           // for(x in y) {...}
177 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
178 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
179 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
180 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
181 |           }
182 |           if (lhs.isName() && !conditional) {
183 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(lhs.getString(), output);
184 |           }
185 |           computeMayUse(rhs, cfgNode, output, conditional);
186 |         }
187 |         return;
188 | 
189 |       case Token.AND:
190 |       case Token.OR:
191 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
192 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
193 |         return;
194 | 
195 |       case Token.HOOK:
196 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
197 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
198 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
199 |         return;
200 | 
201 |       case Token.VAR:
202 |         Node varName = n.getFirstChild();
203 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasChildren(), "AST should be normalized");
204 | 
205 |         if (varName.hasChildren()) {
206 |           computeMayUse(varName.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
207 |           if (!conditional) {
208 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(varName.getString(), output);
209 |           }
210 |         }
211 |         return;
212 | 
213 |       default:
214 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
215 |           Node name = n.getFirstChild();
216 |           if (!conditional) {
217 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(name.getString(), output);
218 |           }
219 | 
220 |           // In case of a += "Hello". There is a read of a.
221 |           if (!n.isAssign()) {
222 |             addToUseIfLocal(name.getString(), cfgNode, output);
223 |           }
224 | 
225 |           computeMayUse(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
226 |         } else {
227 |           /*
228 |            * We want to traverse in reverse order because we want the LAST
229 |            * definition in the sub-tree....
230 |            * But we have no better way to traverse in reverse other :'(
231 |            */
232 |           for (Node c = n.getLastChild(); c != null; c = n.getChildBefore(c)) {
233 |             computeMayUse(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
234 |           }
235 |         }
236 |     }
237 |   }

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.checkRightOf(Node,Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 460-470
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the right of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * NotChecked(), NotChecked(), n, Checked(), Checked();
   */
Method code:
460 |   private static boolean checkRightOf(
461 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
462 |     for (Node p = n; p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
463 |       for (Node cur = p.getNext(); cur != null; cur = cur.getNext()) {
464 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
465 |           return true;
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |     return false;
470 |   }

Original range: 139-153

Method code:
139 |   private void discoverBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> u) {
140 |     u.setAnnotation(GRAY);
141 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : u.getOutEdges()) {
142 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
143 |         continue;
144 |       }
145 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> v = e.getDestination();
146 |       if (v.getAnnotation() == WHITE) {
147 |         discoverBackEdges(v);
148 |       } else if (v.getAnnotation() == GRAY) {
149 |         e.setAnnotation(BACK_EDGE);
150 |       }
151 |     }
152 |     u.setAnnotation(BLACK);
153 |   }

===== M0766 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 166-189
Documentation:

Method code:
166 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
167 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
168 |                                    String sourceString,
169 |                                    Config config,
170 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
171 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
172 |         config, errorReporter);
173 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
174 | 
175 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
176 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
177 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
178 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
179 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
180 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
181 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
182 |         }
183 |       }
184 |     }
185 | 
186 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
187 |

===== M0778 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0781 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1014-1093
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1014 |   private static String strEscape(
1015 |       String s, char quote,
1016 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1017 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1018 |       String backslashEscape,
1019 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1020 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1021 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1022 |     sb.append(quote);
1023 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1024 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1025 |       switch (c) {
1026 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1027 |         case '\u000B':
1028 |           if (useSlashV) {
1029 |             sb.append("\\v");
1030 |           } else {
1031 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1032 |           }
1033 |           break;
1034 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1035 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1036 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1037 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1038 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1039 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1040 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1041 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1042 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1043 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1044 |             sb.append("\\>");
1045 |           } else {
1046 |             sb.append(c);
1047 |           }
1048 |           break;
1049 |         case '<':
1050 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1051 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1052 | 
1053 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1054 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1055 | 
1056 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1057 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1058 |             sb.append("<\\");
1059 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1060 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1061 |             sb.append("<\\");
1062 |           } else {
1063 |             sb.append(c);
1064 |           }
1065 |           break;
1066 |         default:
1067 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1068 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1069 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1070 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1071 |               sb.append(c);
1072 |             } else {
1073 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1074 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1075 |             }
1076 |           } else {
1077 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1078 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1079 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1080 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1081 |               sb.append(c);
1082 |             } else {
1083 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1084 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1085 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1086 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1087 |             }
1088 |           }
1089 |       }
1090 |     }
1091 |     sb.append(quote);
1092 |     return sb.toString();
1093 |   }

===== M0811 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0847 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-730
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         add("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         add("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         add("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n);
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       default:
726 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
727 |     }
728 | 
729 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
730 |   }

===== M0852 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.canInline()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 250-370
Documentation:

Method code:
250 |     private boolean canInline() {
251 | 
252 |       // Cannot inline a parameter.
253 |       if (defCfgNode.isFunction()) {
254 |         return false;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       getDefinition(defCfgNode, null);
258 |       getNumUseInUseCfgNode(useCfgNode, null);
259 | 
260 |       // Definition was not found.
261 |       if (def == null) {
262 |         return false;
263 |       }
264 | 
265 |       // Check that the assignment isn't used as a R-Value.
266 |       // TODO(user): Certain cases we can still inline.
267 |       if (def.isAssign() && !NodeUtil.isExprAssign(def.getParent())) {
268 |         return false;
269 |       }
270 | 
271 | 
272 |       // The right of the definition has side effect:
273 |       // Example, for x:
274 |       // x = readProp(b), modifyProp(b); print(x);
275 |       if (checkRightOf(def, defCfgNode, SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
276 |         return false;
277 |       }
278 | 
279 |       // Similar check as the above but this time, all the sub-expressions
280 |       // left of the use of the variable.
281 |       // x = readProp(b); modifyProp(b), print(x);
282 |       if (checkLeftOf(use, useCfgNode, SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
283 |         return false;
284 |       }
285 | 
286 | 
287 |       // TODO(user): Side-effect is ok sometimes. As long as there are no
288 |       // side-effect function down all paths to the use. Once we have all the
289 |       // side-effect analysis tool.
290 |       if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(def.getLastChild())) {
291 |         return false;
292 |       }
293 | 
294 |       // TODO(user): We could inline all the uses if the expression is short.
295 | 
296 |       // Finally we have to make sure that there are no more than one use
297 |       // in the program and in the CFG node. Even when it is semantically
298 |       // correctly inlining twice increases code size.
299 |       if (numUseWithinUseCfgNode != 1) {
300 |         return false;
301 |       }
302 | 
303 |       // Make sure that the name is not within a loop
304 |       if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(use)) {
305 |         return false;
306 |       }
307 | 
308 | 
309 |       Collection<Node> uses = reachingUses.getUses(varName, defCfgNode);
310 | 
311 |       if (uses.size() != 1) {
312 |         return false;
313 |       }
314 | 
315 |       // We give up inling stuff with R-Value that has GETPROP, GETELEM,
316 |       // or anything that creates a new object.
317 |       // Example:
318 |       // var x = a.b.c; j.c = 1; print(x);
319 |       // Inlining print(a.b.c) is not safe consider j and be alias to a.b.
320 |       // TODO(user): We could get more accuracy by looking more in-detail
321 |       // what j is and what x is trying to into to.
322 |       if (NodeUtil.has(def.getLastChild(),
323 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
324 |               @Override
325 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
326 |                 switch (input.getType()) {
327 |                   case Token.GETELEM:
328 |                   case Token.GETPROP:
329 |                   case Token.ARRAYLIT:
330 |                   case Token.OBJECTLIT:
331 |                   case Token.REGEXP:
332 |                   case Token.NEW:
333 |                     return true;
334 |                 }
335 |                 return false;
336 |               }
337 |           },
338 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
339 |               @Override
340 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
341 |                 // Recurse if the node is not a function.
342 |                 return !input.isFunction();
343 |               }
344 |           })) {
345 |         return false;
346 |       }
347 | 
348 |       // We can skip the side effect check along the paths of two nodes if
349 |       // they are just next to each other.
350 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(defCfgNode.getParent()) &&
351 |           defCfgNode.getNext() != useCfgNode) {
352 |         // Similar side effect check as above but this time the side effect is
353 |         // else where along the path.
354 |         // x = readProp(b); while(modifyProp(b)) {}; print(x);
355 |         CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>
356 |           pathCheck = new CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
357 |                  cfg,
358 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(defCfgNode),
359 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(useCfgNode),

/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.

Method code:
350 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
351 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
352 |     if (n.isFunction()) {

354 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.

===== M0856 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0863 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

===== M0864 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }
